## Executive Summary

The implementation is organized cleanly and the repository quality gate passes all 1,699 tests, lint, formatting, lifecycle, and contract-source checks. It is not ready to ship: the public replay path cannot satisfy frame consistency because it substitutes configuration defaults that are not present in the log, and the report path accepts artifacts that violate the registry while treating JSON files as registry-declared Parquet. The browser-facing acceptance tests inspect source strings instead of executing the page, allowing several broken E6 controls and views to pass.

These values affect `reserved_units` and `margin_ratio_bp`. `ExperimentConfig` permits different prices, fees, multipliers, and per-agent leverage tiers. ---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""CI绿"具体指什么,不要用<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="3438" />要在中间把每个子步骤都抛给用户确认:

1. `git add -A && git commit`:回写后的 `RETROSPECTIVE.md` + `CURRENT-<type>.md`
   最终版一起提交(CURRENT 文件此时保留,是检视证据)
2. `git push` 触发 CI;`gh run list --limit 1` 拿 run id,`gh run watch <run-id>
   --exit-status` 等到 CI 绿
3. CI 红 → 按第7条 loop 重开为第 N+1 轮,**不删除** CURRENT 文件,本轮不闭环;
   若报错和本次改动无关,先比较本地工具版本与 manifest 声明的版本范围是否一致
4. CI 绿 → 执行第6步删除(`git rm`),随后 `git commit` + `git push`(纯删除提交,
   不重新作为闭环判定依据——闭环已在修复提交的 CI 绿时达成;若仓库要求每个提交
   都绿,push 后顺带确认不红即可)
5. 汇报"检视闭环"并退出检视环节,回到正常开发流程,不留"等用户收尾"

只有两种情况允许停下来问用户:(a) 客观不可执行——缺推送凭据、无 CI 权限、
`gh` 未安装等,此时把卡住的步骤和原因一次性说明;(b) 删除动作与用户明确表达的
意愿冲突。其余时候"要不要提交/要不要删/还要确认吗"式的询问,是把检视人自己该
完成的核对外包给用户当人肉确认点,禁止。

## 9. 与其他 review skill 的关系

- `code-review-graph` 装的 `review-changes` / `debug-issue` / `refactor-safely` /
  `explore-codebase`:这些是**图谱查询原语**,当作本 skill 第2、7步调用的基础
  设施使用,不是并列的独立审查流程
- 通用的一次性 `code-review-agent` 式 skill:适合真正的单次审计(没有后续修复-
  复核循环的场景);一旦审查进入"改了再审"的循环,改用本 skill,不要两边混用
  导致报告格式不一致
- CI 校验(`ci-verify` 之类):是第7条停止条件的一部分,不是独立步骤;作为最终
  门禁只在收敛候选轮触发一次,闭环判定必须把它纳入,不能只看severity清零就宣布
  完成

# MarketGameSim 项目须知

## 项目原则入口

- **不可违反原则**唯一入口：[`docs/SOP.md`](docs/SOP.md)（含各原则唯一拥有者链接）。
- 规格与生命周期规则：[`docs/features/README.md`](docs/features/README.md)。
- 全仓文档所有权地图：[`docs/README.md`](docs/README.md)。
- 当前 v0.1 规格真源：[`docs/features/0.1/spec.md`](docs/features/0.1/spec.md)。

## 提交前必须本地跑通

唯一公开验证入口（真源、生命周期、链接、所有权、pytest、ruff 全部覆盖）：

```bash
python tools/verify.py
```

`verify.py` 失败即返回非零。各底层命令仍可单独用于定位，但不再各自维护完整命令清单。

CI 的 `lint` job 与 `test` job 是独立的两步，`pytest` 全绿不代表 `ruff` 也会通过——
0.1.1 首次提交时就因为没跑 lint，被 CI 的 `ruff check .` 挡下 105 处违规（多数是
超长行、未清理的 import/变量）。提交前在本地跑一遍，几秒钟能挡住，不用等 CI 跑完
再回来改。

`ruff format .` 与 `ruff check . --fix` 能自动处理大部分问题（超长行、未排序/未使用
的 import 等）；剩下的（未使用变量、过宽的异常断言、废弃写法等）需要手工看一眼再改，
改完重新跑一遍 `python tools/verify.py` 确认全绿。

## 推送到远程后必须确认 CI 真的通过，不能推完就当结束

`git push` 成功只代表代码到了远程，不代表 CI 会通过——本地全绿不保证 CI 也
全绿（见下一节的工具版本漂移问题，以及 CI 环境本身和本地终究不是同一个
环境）。每次 `push` 之后用 `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`（或先
`gh run list --limit 1` 拿到本次的 run id）等它跑完，确认全部 job（目前是

# Code Review Report

**Reviewed**: 0.1.4 replay/report specification, implementation, tests, and the `Book.level_aggregates()` oracle support change
**Language(s)**: Python, JavaScript, HTML, JSON, Markdown
**Review Date**: 2026-08-11

## Executive Summary

The implementation is organized cleanly and the repository quality gate passes all 1,699 tests, lint, formatting, lifecycle, and contract-source checks. It is not ready to ship: the public replay path cannot satisfy frame consistency because it substitutes configuration defaults that are not present in the log, and the report path accepts artifacts that violate the registry while treating JSON files as registry-declared Parquet. The browser-facing acceptance tests inspect source strings instead of executing the page, allowing several broken E6 controls and views to pass.

## Findings

### Correctness

#### 🔴 Public replay output cannot satisfy E1 for configurable runs — `src/market_game_sim/replay/generate.py:23`

**Severity**: Critical

**Problem**: `build_replay()` is the specified log-only public entry point, but it rebuilds every run with hard-coded `mult=1000`, `initial_price_ticks=10000`, `fee_bps_cap=0`, and an implicit initial-margin rate of 10000 bp. These values affect `reserved_units` and `margin_ratio_bp`. `ExperimentConfig` permits different prices, fees, multipliers, and per-agent leverage tiers. The E1 integration test avoids the public path and injects the real configuration directly into private `_build_frames()` at `tests/integration/test_replay_frame_consistency.py:165`, so it does not establish that the generated replay is consistent.

**Current Code**:

```python
DEFAULT_MULT = 1000
DEFAULT_INITIAL_PRICE = 10000

log = read_log(log_path)
frames = _build_frames(log.events, mult=DE<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="10017" />d-rules | 非法降采样规则 | Medium | correctness | root-cause | original-coding | fixed | __post_init__ 校验 + CLI 错误退出 | `test_downsampling.py` 5 条 | 1 | 2 | — |
| negative-results-shape-conflict | negative_results 形状冲突 | Medium | correctness | root-cause | spec-drift | fixed | design.md §4 改对象（verbatim） | 字节一致断言保留 | 1 | 2 | — |
| acceptance-tests-prove-markers | 验收测试只证 marker | Medium | test-coverage | root-cause | process-gap | fixed | E1 走公共路径 + 真实 table 夹具；浏览器由 E2/T403 手动 | 公共路径 2 条 + 多行夹具 | 1 | 2 | test-simulates-itself |
| report-pair-atomicity | 报告未成对原子发布 | Low | quality | root-cause | original-coding | fixed | 专属临时目录+fsync+如实文档 | rename 失败 + 无残留 | 1 | 2 | — |

**模式性教训**: 14 条中 `original-coding` 12、`spec-drift` 1、`process-gap` 1；无
`fix-regression`（round 2 修复自伤率为 0——但这不是"修复没引入新问题"，而是本轮把
子代理的两处遗漏在主检视内补掉，且修复 diff 都配了正反回归测试）。**两条 Critical
都属同一根因的两半**：公开入口把配置当第二真源硬编码（`duplicate-source-of-truth`），
报告层用 marker/夹具冒充真行为验收（`test-simulates-itself`）——这印证了 round 1
结论「不是 ready to ship」的本质：不是缺功能，是"看到的"与"被验证的"脱节。修复的
通用抓手是**把配置写进日志头让日志成为唯一真源**、**把验收从 marker 换成公共路径 +
真实格式夹具**。另有一处 `trust-boundary-escape`（artifact 路径逃逸）说明完整性/信任
边界（artifact_root）必须有显式限制，不能只靠"扫描目录内"这类隐含假设。

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

[build-system]
requires = ["hatchling"]
build-backend = "hatchling.build"

[project]
name = "market-game-sim"
version = "0.1.0"
description = "Reproducible multi-agent limit-order-book market simulation"
readme = "README.md"
requires-python = ">=3.11"
dependencies = ["pyyaml>=6"]

[project.optional-dependencies]
dev = [
  # 固定到 minor 版本：CI 用 `pip install -e ".[dev]"` 每次拉最新，2026-08-09
  # 就因为0.15->0.16的默认行为变化（markdown内嵌代码块格式化默认启用）在没有
  # 任何本仓库代码变更的情况下把CI的ruff job搞红——linter版本漂移不该成为
  # CI失败的来源，锁定范围换取可重复性。上界与本地验证版本保持一致。
  "pytest>=8,<9",
  "pytest-cov>=5,<7",
  "ruff>=0.16,<0.17",
]

[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]
packages = ["src/market_game_sim"]

[tool.pytest.ini_options]
addopts = "-ra --strict-markers"
testpaths = ["tests"]

[tool.ruff]
line-length = 100
target-version = "py311"
# docs/、conversations/ 下的 .md 文件里大量引用/粘贴的代码片段仅供阅读，不是
# 维护中的源码——用ruff format重排它们既无意义，又会破坏对话存档/复盘报告的
# 历史原样性。ruff check（lint）本身不处理markdown，这条只影响 format。
extend-exclude = ["*.md"]

[tool.ruff.lint]
select = ["E", "F", "I", "B", "UP", "SIM"]

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""CI绿"具体指什么,不要<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="7801" />断网环境
  打开产物、功能完整、无控制台报错）。

 728: #### 4.6.3 强制初态快照（两个真正的队列事件）
 729: 
 730: **内核在 `timestamp = 0` 预先入队两个 `SNAPSHOT` 队列事件**：先 `ACCOUNT`
 731: （`enqueue_seq = 0`）后 `BOOK`（`enqueue_seq = 1`）。它们像其他队列事件一样弹出，
 732: 各自形成一个事务、各自 `record_index = 0`。**业务事务从两个快照之后开始。**
 733: 
 734: ##### bootstrap 屏障（必须显式实现，`enqueue_seq` 不足以保证）
 735: 
 736: **`enqueue_seq` 只裁决「同 `timestamp` 且同 `priority_class`」的先后。** queue key 是
 737: `(timestamp, priority_class, enqueue_seq)`，**先比 class**——因此若 `t = 0` 时队列里
 738: 已存在任何 class 0—4 的业务事件，那两个 class 5 的快照会排在它们**后面**，
 739: 「日志前两条恒为初态快照」根本不成立。
 740: 
 741: 因此 bootstrap 是一条**调度约束**，不是靠优先级自然发生的：
 742: 
 743: > **内核启动时队列中只有这两个 `SNAPSHOT` 事件。任何业务事件（含代理的首次
 744: > `AGENT_OBSERVE`、做市商的首次报价）的入队，都发生在两者都提交之后。**
 745: 
 746: 这条约束落在**入队时点**上，与 queue key 的比较规则无关，因此不受 class 影响。
 747: 实现须提供断言：bootstrap 未完成时调用入队接口即抛异常（配 `abort_code = INTERNAL`）。
 748: 
 749: **已知缺口（2026-08-11 记录，见 ADR-004）**：当前内核实现未强制该屏障——实验运行器
 750: 在 `bootstrap()` 后立即入队各代理的首次 `AGENT_OBSERVE`（class 3 < 5），因此真实日志
 751: 中两个快照落在 `transaction_seq = b, b+1`（`b = 1 + t=0 低 class 事件数`），而非固定
 752: `1, 2`。回放读取器按以下**可判定规则**强制快照结构（TI-5）：前<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="511" />unit/replay/test_log_reader.py`

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

Keep browser work in the background by default.

Show the browser when the user's request is primarily to put a page in front of them or let them watch the interaction, such as "open localhost:3000", "go to the docs page", "take me to the PR", "show me the current tab", or "keep the browser open while you test checkout".

Do not show the browser when navigation is only a means to answer a question or verify behavior, such as "check localhost:3000 and tell me whether login works", "inspect the docs page and summarize what changed", or "verify the modal still opens correctly". Localhost targets and ordinary page navigation do not by themselves require visibility.

When the browser should be visible to the user, actually present it with `await (await browser.capabilities.get("visibility")).set(true)`.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

# MarketGameSim 项目须知

## 项目原则入口

- **不可违反原则**唯一入口：[`docs/SOP.md`](docs/SOP.md)（含各原则唯一拥有者链接）。
- 规格与生命周期规则：[`docs/features/README.md`](docs/features/README.md)。
- 全仓文档所有权地图：[`docs/README.md`](docs/README.md)。
- 当前 v0.1 规格真源：[`docs/features/0.1/spec.md`](docs/features/0.1/spec.md)。

## 提交前必须本地跑通

唯一公开验证入口（真源、生命周期、链接、所有权、pytest、ruff 全部覆盖）：

```bash
python tools/verify.py
```

`verify.py` 失败即返回非零。各底层命令仍可单独用于定位，但不再各自维护完整命令清单。

CI 的 `lint` job 与 `test` job 是独立的两步，`pytest` 全绿不代表 `ruff` 也会通过——
0.1.1 首次提交时就因为没跑 lint，被 CI 的 `ruff check .` 挡下 105 处违规（多数是
超长行、未清理的 import/变量）。提交前在本地跑一遍，几秒钟能挡住，不用等 CI 跑完
再回来改。

`ruff format .` 与 `ruff check . --fix` 能自动处理大部分问题（超长行、未排序/未使用
的 import 等）；剩下的（未使用变量、过宽的异常断言、废弃写法等）需要手工看一眼再改，
改完重新跑一遍 `python tools/verify.py` 确认全绿。

## 推送到远程后必须确认 CI 真的通过，不能推完就当结束

`git push` 成功只代表代码到了远程，不代表 CI 会通过——本地全绿不保证 CI 也
全绿（见下一节的工具版本漂移问题，以及 CI 环境本身和本地终究不是同一个
环境）。每次 `push` 之后用 `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`（或先
`gh run list --limit 1` 拿到本次的 run id）等它跑完，确认全部 job（目前是
真源与生命周期校验、`ruff`、`pytest` 3.11、`pytest` 3.13，共 4 个）都<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2891" />法逐帧拖拽 | High | 正确性 | 根因 | 修复回归 | open | 保留唯一帧位置并单独显示逻辑时间 | 同 timestamp 多帧可达性测试 | 4 | — | non-unique-key-mapping |

  仍由 `test_report_artifacts.py` 按同一 registry 动态构造；上游 producer 没有 JSON
  输出与 manifest E2E。把 label 从 parquet 改成 json 尚未证明报告能消费真实冻结产物。
- **日志负例仍被接受**：实测两个 EVENT 均缺 `schema_version/run_id`、trailer 只含

  仍写 txn 1/2、k+2，日志结构图还写“其余事务从 3 开始”。
- **timeline 修复破坏任意帧可达性**：`frameForTimestamp` 对 `[0,0,10]` 和目标时间 0
  返回 frame 1，frame 0 无法由 slider 到达；同时间戳多事务是内核正常且常见的形状。
- **确认修复**：schema v2/未来版本已拒绝且 v3 接受；零匹配降采样已在写出前拒绝；
  原 `frame-missing-timestamp` 已完成 timestamp 展示与范围接线。
- **浏览器验收**：再次按 browser skill 尝试连接，但环境缺少沙箱元数据，无法启动；

"""T101 (FR-019): Independent event-log reader.

Parses the three top-level record kinds (``RUN_HEADER`` + ``EVENT*`` +
``RUN_TRAILER``) from a JSONL log file WITHOUT importing ``kernel/`` or
``eventlog/``.  Rejects TI-4/TI-5 logs (degenerate-states.md §4).

Termination discrimination is structural first (TI-5), then semantic (TI-4):
a structurally broken log never has its ``terminated`` field trusted.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import pathlib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any


class LogError(Exception):
    """Raised when a log cannot be read or is TI-4/TI-5 invalid."""


@dataclass
class ReplayConfig:
    """Replay-critical config parsed from the RUN_HEADER (F1).

    These four values are needed to rebuild ``reserved_units`` and
    ``margin_ratio_bp`` per frame; they are not derivable from the event
    stream alone (ADR-001 forbids float derivation), so they travel in the
    header to guarantee the public ``build_replay`` path is E1-consistent.
    """

    mult: int = 1000
    fee_bps_cap: int = 0
    initial_price_ticks: int = 10000
    agent_initial_bp: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @classmethod
    def from_header(cls, header: dict[str, Any]) -> ReplayConfig:
        required = ("mult", "fee_bps_cap", "initial_price_ticks", "agent_initial_bp")
        missing = [k for k in required if k not in header]
        if missing:
            raise LogError(f"TI-5: RUN_HEADER missing replay-critical fields: {missing}")
        if not isinstance(header["mult"], int) or isinstance(header["mult"], bool):
            raise LogError("TI-5: RUN_HEADER.mult must be int")
        if not isinsta<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="9039" />ditional_conclusion.json"

class TestFormatContract:
    """R-A: the registry format is the consumption contract -- every artifact
    is json (JSON table/object); parquet is reserved and not consumable."""

    def test_parquet_declared_manifest_entry_rejected(self, tmp_path):
        """A manifest entry declaring format=parquet (registry says json) must
        fail with FIELD_SCHEMA_INVALID at manifest validation."""
        manifest_path, _root, _contents = _build_realistic_setup(tmp_path)
        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for e in manifest["artifacts"]:
            if e["artifact_id"] == "market_metrics":
                e["format"] = "parquet"
        manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest), encoding="utf-8")
        result = build_report(manifest_path, tmp_path / "out")
        assert result.success is False
        assert result.report["failure"]["code"] == "FIELD_SCHEMA_INVALID"

    def test_json_table_artifact_consumed(self, tmp_path):
        """Accepted side: a json table artifact (the only declared format) is
        consumed and appears byte-identical in the report."""
        manifest_path, _root, contents = _build_realistic_setup(tmp_path)
        result = build_report(manifest_path, tmp_path / "out")
        assert result.success is True
        assert result.report["metrics"]["market_metrics"] == contents["market_metrics"]

docs/contracts/event-schema.md:763:| 零业务事务的正常运行 | `terminated=COMPLETED`，`last_committed_transaction_seq = 2`，恰 2 条 EVENT |

docs/contracts/event-schema.md:930:  **阶段 2** 仅在阶段 1 全通过后执行，`terminated = ABORTED` → 判 **TI-4**。

"""T201 (AC-002, E2/PR-018): single-file offline replay acceptance.

Generates a replay HTML from a real small run's log and asserts it is a
single self-contained file with no external requests.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

from market_game_sim.agent.scheduler import AgentSpec
from market_game_sim.experiment.runner import ExperimentConfig, run_one
from market_game_sim.ledger.account import initial_margin_bp_for_tier
from market_game_sim.replay.generate import build_replay


def _mm_spec() -> AgentSpec:
    return AgentSpec(
        agent_id="mm-0",
        role="inventory_market_maker",
        observe_interval_ns=100_000_000,
        latency_ns=5_000_000,
        is_market_maker=True,
        half_spread_ticks=5,
        quote_size=10_000,
        max_inventory=100_000,
        inventory_skew_k_bp=10_000,
    )


def _belief_spec() -> AgentSpec:
    return AgentSpec(
        agent_id="agent-0",
        role="retail",
        observe_interval_ns=1_000_000_000,
        latency_ns=50_000_000,
        leverage_tier=10,
        aggressiveness_bp=5_000,
        max_order_qty=5_000,
    )


def _write_log(path, result, config: ExperimentConfig) -> None:
    header = {
        "record_kind": "RUN_HEADER",
        "schema_version": 3,
        "run_id": f"exp-s{result.seed}",
        "tick_size": "0.01",
        "min_quantity": "0.001",
        "cash_unit": "0.01",
        "mult": config.mult,
        "fee_bps_cap": max(config.maker_bps, config.taker_bps, 0),
        "initial_price_ticks": config.initial_price_ticks,
        "agent_initial_bp": {
            s.agent_id: initial_margin_bp_for_tier(s.leverage_tier) for s in config.agent_specs
        },
    }
    max_txn = max((e["transaction_seq"] for e in result.events), default=2)
    trailer = {
        "record_kind": "RUN_TRAILER",
        "terminated": result.terminated,
        "abort_code": result.abort_code,
        "last_committed_transaction_seq": max_txn,
        "record_count": 2 + len(result.events),
    }
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(header) + "\n")
        for e in result.events:
            f.write(json.dumps(e) + "\n")
        f.write(json.dumps(trailer) + "\n")


def test_offline_single_file_generated(tmp_path):
    cfg = ExperimentConfig(
        seed=1,
        max_transactions=60,
        agent_specs=[_mm_spec(), _belief_spec()],
        agent_signals={"agent-0": 10_000},
    )
    result = run_one(cfg)
    assert result.terminated == "COMPLETED"

    log_path = tmp_path / "run.jsonl"
    out_path = tmp_path / "replay.html"
    _write_log(log_path, result, cfg)
    build_replay(log_path, out_path)

    html = out_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert html.count("<html") == 1
    assert "replay-data" in html
    assert "fetch(" not in html
    assert "http://" not in html
    assert "https://" not in html
    assert "cdn" not in html.lower()
    assert not (tmp_path / "replay.html.tmp").exists()


def test_generated_html_contains_run_id(tmp_path):
    cfg = ExperimentConfig(
        seed=1,
        max_transactions=40,
        agent_specs=[_mm_spec(), _belief_spec()],
        agent_signals={"agent-0": 10_000},
    )
    result = run_one(cfg)
    log_path = tmp_path / "run.jsonl"
    _write_log(log_path, result, cfg)
    out_path = tmp_path / "replay.html"
    build_replay(log_path, out_path)
    html = out_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert f"exp-s{result.seed}" in html

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

    liqMarkTextAtLiqFrame = await page.textContent('#liq-marks');
  }

  const canvases = {};
  for (const id of ['price-canvas', 'kline-canvas', 'book-canvas', 'account-canvas']) {

  const result = {
    offline_mode: true,
    frame_info_initial: frameInfo0,
    pause_button_text: pauseBtnText,
    pause_holds_frame: pauseHolds,
    frame_after_speed_change: frameInfoSpeed,
    frame_after_seek_to_end: frameInfoSeek,
    liquidation_frames_in_data: liqFrames,

    canvases_present: canvases,

    console_errors: consoleErrors,

  // Full conjunction with explicit parentheses -- NO || shortcut may bypass

    Object.values(canvases).every(Boolean) &&
    nonFileRequests.length === 0 &&
    consoleErrors.length === 0 &&
    pageErrors.length === 0;

  console.log('T403_RESULT ' + JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
  await browser.close();
  process.exit(pass ? 0 : 1);

"""T606 (0.1.3 §4): negative results as first-class products.

Promotes the three negative-result classes -- narrow parameter region,
effect vanishing under an alternative mapping, and crash without leverage --
to first-class products.  Body, abstract and machine-readable conclusion
must agree; they are never relegated to an appendix.

These are *valid* outputs (0.1.3 §4), not failures: they narrow where the
claim holds or negate it, which is exactly what preregistration protects.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

NEGATIVE_RESULT_CLASSES = (
    "narrow_parameter_region",
    "effect_vanishes_under_alternative_mapping",
    "crash_without_leverage",
)


class NegativeResultError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when negative results are omitted from body/abstract/conclusion."""


@dataclass
class NegativeResult:
    result_class: str
    description: str
    machine_readable: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def validate(self) -> list[str]:
        problems: list[str] = []
        if self.result_class not in NEGATIVE_RESULT_CLASSES:
            problems.append(f"unknown negative-result class {self.result_class}")
        return problems


@dataclass
class NegativeResultReport:
    results: list[NegativeResult] = field(default_factory=list)

    def validate(self) -> None:
        """Fail-closed: every negative result must appear identically in the
        body, abstract and machine-readable conclusion (not just appendix)."""
        for r in self.results:
            problems = r.validate()
            if problems:
                raise NegativeResultError("; ".join(problems))
            if not r.description:
                raise NegativeResultError(
                    f"negative result {r.result_class} has no body description"
                )
            if not r.<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2247" />gregation/dedup/artifact paths, NOT as pairing join keys

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""CI绿"具体指什么,不要用<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="6632" />- T606 门禁：方向反转 cells → `effect_vanishes_under_alternative_mapping`（封闭枚举）。
- **待办**：CI 最终门禁（commit/push 后 `gh run watch` 确认 4 job 全绿）。

        mappings=mappings,
        structure_desc="2xMM+1xretail",

"""T105 (0.1.3 E1): model-family x behavior-mapping cross matrix.

Builds the full ``model_family_id × behavior_mapping_id`` cross-contrast
matrix: every pre-registered model family runs *every* pre-registered
behavior mapping (not a sampled subset), each cell on a common comparable
parameter point and seed set, forming a single-dimension pairing.

The report declares mapping main effect, family main effect and their
interaction / direction-reversal.  "同向成立" (same-direction robustness) holds
only when the *whole* matrix is directionally consistent -- not when the two
dimensions are counted separately and stitched together.  Any reversal or
non-significance is reported as a dependency boundary (E1's "明确报告" branch).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any


class CrossMatrixError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised on an incomplete or directionally inconsistent cross matrix."""


@dataclass
class CrossCell:
    family_id: str
    mapping_id: str
    effect_direction: int  # +1 / -1 / 0
    significant: bool
    effect_size: float = 0.0
    ci_low: float = 0.0
    ci_high: float = 0.0


@dataclass
class CrossMatrix:
    cells: list[CrossCell] = field(default_factory=list)

    def cell(self, family_id: str, mapping_id: str) -> CrossCell | None:
        for c in self.cells:
            if c.family_id == family_id and c.mapping_id == mapping_id:
                return c
        return None

    def dimensions(self) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
        families = sorted({c.family_id for c in self.cells})
        mappings = sorted({c.mapping_id for c in self.cells})
        return families, mappings

    def is_complete(self, families: list[str], mappings: list[str]) -> bool:

name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

  narrow→narrow_parameter_region（3 条全过）。

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

---
name: ci-verify
description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
---

## CI Verify

A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
dependency resolution, different tool versions.

### Before committing

1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
   `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
   workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
   the ground truth for what "passing" means.
2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
   whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
   push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
   `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
   differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
   diagnostic note below.

### After pushing

1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
   workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
   poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

 236:    最终版一起提交(CURRENT 文件此时保留,是检视证据)
 237: 2. `git push` 触发 CI;`gh run list --limit 1` 拿 run id,`gh run watch <run-id>
 238:    --exit-status` 等到 CI 绿
 239: 3. CI 红 → 按第7条 loop 重开为第 N+1 轮,**不删除** CURRENT 文件,本轮不闭环;
 240:    若报错和本次改动无关,先比较本地工具版本与 manifest 声明的版本范围是否一致
 241: 4. CI 绿 → 执行第6步删除(`git rm`),随后 `git commit` + `git push`(纯删除提交,
 242:    不重新作为闭环判定依据——闭环已在修复提交的 CI 绿时达成;若仓库要求每个提交
 243:    都绿,push 后顺带确认不红即可)
 244: 5. 汇报"检视闭环"并退出检视环节,回到正常开发流程,不留"等用户收尾"
 245: 
 246: 只有两种情况允许停下来问用户:(a) 客观不可执行——缺推送凭据、无 CI 权限、
 247: `gh` 未安装等,此时把卡住的步骤和原因一次性说明;(b) 删除动作与用户明确表达的
 248: 意愿冲突。其余时候"要不要提交/要不要删/还要确认吗"式的询问,是把检视人自己该
 249: 完成的核对外包给用户当人肉确认点,禁止。

---
name: conversation-archive
description: >
  归档本机 AI CLI（OpenCode、Claude Code、Codex CLI）在某个项目里的全部对话记录，
  生成结构化的 Markdown 会话文件与血缘树/时间线/复盘概览视图，供项目结束后用 AI 做
  深度复盘。当用户说「归档/整理/总结 AI 对话记录」「把会话推送/保存到仓库」「生成会话
  复盘/索引/时间线」「项目收尾做复盘」，或想还原「A 写代码 → B 检视 → A 再改」这类
  多代理穿插协作时使用。也适用于任何新项目想持续记录开发对话以支持事后复盘。
compatibility: Python 3.11+, ruff（可选，仅做代码检查）；需已安装对应 CLI（OpenCode/Claude Code/Codex）
---

# AI 对话归档（Conversation Archive）

把某项目在本机三大 AI CLI（OpenCode / Claude Code / Codex CLI）中的全部会话归档成
Markdown，并生成供 AI 复盘用的血缘树、时间线、复盘概览三个视图。数据源与目标项目
解耦：脚本自动从用户主目录定位各 CLI 数据，项目路径通过参数指定（默认当前工作目录），
因此**可直接复用于任意新项目**。

## 两个脚本

本 skill 携带两个可移植脚本（在 `scripts/` 下）：

| 脚本 | 作用 |
|---|---|
| `export_conversations.py` | 从各 CLI 原始数据导出会话 md（原始数据 → 会话文件） |
| `build_retrospective.py` | 从会话文件生成三个视图（会话文件 → index/timeline/retrospective） |

数据源位置（自动从 `Path.home()` 定位，跨平台）：
- OpenCode：`~/.local/share/opencode/opencode.db`（SQLite）
- Claude Code：`~/.claude/projects/<project-encoded>/*.jsonl`
- Codex CLI：`~/.codex/sessions/**/rollout-*.jsonl`

## 工作流

在目标项目目录下执行（当前工作目录即目标项目<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="189" />发/收集等）
├── retrospective.md    # 按主工作流分组的复盘概览（工具/模型/token/成本/子代理）
├── opencode/           # OpenCode 会话
├── claude/             # Claude Code 会话
└── codex/              # Codex CLI 会话
```

## 维护：幂等全量重导

项目未完成、会话会持续增长时，无需任何增量逻辑——每次全量重导，天然幂等：

1. `export` 每次清空各工具子目录再全量重写会话（秒级），新增会话后重跑即纳入；
2. `build` 从会话 frontmatter 重建全部视图，与数据源解耦，任何新会话自动进入各视图。

这保证了归档始终与最新会话保持一致，零手工维护。

## 复盘用法

让 AI 以 `conversations/index.md` 的血缘树定位工作流，用 `timeline-summary.md` 还原
多代理穿插次序，用 `retrospective.md` 看每个工作流的工作量与派生子代理；需要细节时
再深入对应会话文件。frontmatter 的 token / cost / model 可做工作量与成本分析。

## 会话文件格式

每个会话 `*.md` 含 YAML frontmatter（`tool` / `model` / `created_at` / `updated_at` /
`tokens_input` / `tokens_output` / `cost`，及可选的 `parent_id`、`thread_id`）与结构化
正文（`## user/assistant` 分段、`- **tool**:` 工具调用、`<details>` 折叠工具输出）。

## 编排级事件识别

`build_retrospective.py` 内置 `ORCHESTRATION_TOOLS` 集合（task/spawn/agent/followup/
background_output/background_cancel 等），`--summary` 时只保留这些编排级事件 + 会话
起止，用于快速呈现「A 写 → B 检视 → A 改」的穿插协作。需要增删编排工具时改这一处即可。

## 代码质量

脚本遵循 PEP8，可运行 `ruff check <script>` / `ruff format --check <script>` 校验。
若项目根目录是 git 仓库，建议在 `.gitignore` 中按需决定是否纳入 `conversations/`。

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.
---

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""C<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4399" />
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.

# MarketGameSim 项目须知

## 项目原则入口

- **不可违反原则**唯一入口：[`docs/SOP.md`](docs/SOP.md)（含各原则唯一拥有者链接）。
- 规格与生命周期规则：[`docs/features/README.md`](docs/features/README.md)。
- 全仓文档所有权地图：[`docs/README.md`](docs/README.md)。
- 当前 v0.1 规格真源：[`docs/features/0.1/spec.md`](docs/features/0.1/spec.md)。

## 提交前必须本地跑通

唯一公开验证入口（真源、生命周期、链接、所有权、pytest、ruff 全部覆盖）：

```bash
python tools/verify.py
```

`verify.py` 失败即返回非零。各底层命令仍可单独用于定位，但不再各自维护完整命令清单。

CI 的 `lint` job 与 `test` job 是独立的两步，`pytest` 全绿不代表 `ruff` 也会通过——
0.1.1 首次提交时就因为没跑 lint，被 CI 的 `ruff check .` 挡下 105 处违规（多数是
超长行、未清理的 import/变量）。提交前在本地跑一遍，几秒钟能挡住，不用等 CI 跑完
再回来改。

`ruff format .` 与 `ruff check . --fix` 能自动处理大部分问题（超长行、未排序/未使用
的 import 等）；剩下的（未使用变量、过宽的异常断言、废弃写法等）需要手工看一眼再改，
改完重新跑一遍 `python tools/verify.py` 确认全绿。

## 推送到远程后必须确认 CI 真的通过，不能推完就当结束

`git push` 成功只代表代码到了远程，不代表 CI 会通过——本地全绿不保证 CI 也
全绿（见下一节的工具版本漂移问题，以及 CI 环境本身和本地终究不是同一个
环境）。每次 `push` 之后用 `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`（或先
`gh run list --limit 1` 拿到本次的 run id）等它跑完，确认全部 job（目前是
真源与生命周期校验、`ruff`、`py<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1429" />ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7

        with:
          python-version: "3.11"
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      - run: ruff check .
      - run: ruff format --check .

  test:
    name: pytest (${{ matrix.python-version }})
    needs: contract-sources
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        # 下限锁 3.11（requires-python）；上限跟随，用于尽早发现新版本的行为差异。
        # NFR-001 要求逐事件一致，跨版本差异必须在实现阶段就暴露，而不是实验阶段。
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.13"]
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v7
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"
      # PYTHONHASHSEED=0 只是【让普通测试可复现】，**不是**检测误用内置 hash() 的手段。
      # 恰恰相反：固定 seed 会让一个误用 hash() 的实现在每次 CI 中稳定通过，
      # 把跨进程随机盐这个缺陷藏起来（第 36 章 P1-U03）。
      #
      # 真正的确定性门必须【跨两个不同 seed 的独立进程】比较同一运行的事件摘要，
      # 两者不等即失败。它需要哈希实现存在才能建立，由 0.1.1 T602 / 退出条件 E4 负责。
      #
      # 覆盖率门槛：语句+分支合并覆盖率 ≥90%（T001b：87% 提升至 90% 后实测 91%）。
      - env:
          PYTHONHASHSEED: "0"

---
name: review-convergence
description: >
  Structured, convergence-driven code/document review protocol for iterative fix-verify
  cycles — not a one-shot audit. USE FOR: code review, review my code, 代码检视, 复核,
  审查这段改动, check this PR, verify the fix, is this ready to merge, 检视报告, fix
  verification round, re-review after fixes, audit this diff. Triggers whenever a review
  is expected to span more than one round (a fix gets re-checked, a PR gets updated, a
  "round N" verification is requested) or when the repo has CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md testing
  conventions that a review must enforce. TAKES PRECEDENCE over generic single-pass
  code-review skills (including any local `code-review-agent`-style skill) whenever the
  task is part of a multi-round review-fix loop — those skills are one-shot report
  generators and have no exit condition, which is exactly the failure mode this skill
  fixes. Falls back to letting a generic skill handle truly one-off, non-iterative audits
  with no follow-up expected.

# Review Convergence

十轮审查还能挑出中等问题、修复还大概率引入新问题——这不是审查不够仔细,是流程没有
收敛条件。"审到审查者没意见为止"不是良定义的停止条件:只要投入足够注意力,任何代码
都能挑出新问题。本skill的目的是让审查在有限轮次内收敛,而不是无限发散。

## 0. 项目适配(每次先做,不要跳过)

在开始之前,读取当前仓库的 `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md`(如果存在),提取:
- 该项目自己的测试/回归规范(例如"每次修复必须补充回归测试"这类硬性要求)
- 已知的历史教训(例如"assert 被悄悄降级成 warning"这类具体反面案例)
- 本地校验命令(lint/test/format 分别是什么)

这些内容决定下面各步骤里"回归测试""C<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="4399" />
   to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
   job you didn't think to check).
4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
   log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
   push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
   triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
change just made. Before searching further:

- Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
  the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
  locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
  typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
  loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
  once, months ago — never picked up.
- If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
  range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
  that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
  config adjustments the new version requires.

### Environment note: `gh` unavailable in WSL

If `gh` is not found in your shell but works on Windows (e.g. WSL with the
Windows GitHub CLI installed), don't treat it as a missing credential and
stop to ask the user. Create a symlink into a PATH directory — the Windows
keyring auth is shared, no re-login needed:

```bash
ln -s "/mnt/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe" /mnt/c/Users/<you>/.local/bin/gh
```

Verify once with `gh run list --limit 1`. If the Windows GitHub CLI is not
installed at all, then (and only then) credential provisioning is a real
user-gated step — surface it once with the exact install command instead of
re-prompting per command.
